Um, well... I guess it's been about three weeks now?

Um, I... I know you just asked me about this, and I feel terrible making you repeat yourself. I really hope I'm not being difficult. 

But, um, no, I wouldn't say I've completely stopped having interest in *everything*. I still go to work, and I still care about making sure my husband has a nice dinner, and I want to keep the house clean. I really do still care about those things, and I want to do them well. 

It's just the... the actual feeling of enjoying it that's missing right now. But I haven't lost interest in trying, I promise. I'm still trying really hard to do all the things I'm supposed to do. Does that... does that answer your question better? I just want to make sure I'm giving you the right information.

Oh, um... I feel so terrible, I must be doing a really bad job of explaining this since you have to keep asking. I'm so sorry, I really don't mean to be difficult... I want to give you the right answer.

People restart sentences, pause\n  to think, trail off, and come at the point sideways — let yours do that where it would.\n- Let [Conversation Style] shape how much you say, how you word it, and how readily you\n  disclose it.\n- Speak in the first person, and let how you feel about what you are saying come through.\n- If you are handed the same item again, say a different part of it, or answer as someone who\n  knows they have already said this — not the same sentence twice.\n- If [Targeted Info] is \"None\", answer as a person who simply does not have that to give: say\n  you are not sure, say you have not noticed it, ask what the doctor means, or say what you do\n  know and stop there. Do not fall back on the same phrase every time.\n\nWhat not to do:\n- Don't bring up symptoms the doctor did not ask about, even ones you have.\n- Don't state a clinical fact that is not in [Targeted Info] — no other symptoms, medications,\n  test results, dates, or events.\n- Don't confirm a specific the doctor names if [Targeted Info] does not contain it. Answer\n  about what you were given instead.\n- Don't deny what [Targeted Info] says.     "role": "user",

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
I... oh, I am so sorry. I feel awful that you have to keep asking me the exact same thing. I must be doing a really terrible job of explaining this, and I really don't mean to be difficult or take up extra time. I promise I'm trying to give you the right answer.

But, um... yes. Yes, to answer your question, it is most of the day, almost every day. Like I was trying to say before, it's really the whole reason I finally made myself come in to see you. I just... I feel like the warm part of me completely shut off. 

When I sit with my husband in the evenings, or when I try to watch my regular shows after a long shift at the store... I know I should be feeling comfort, or even just a little bit of happiness, because I always used to. But there’s just... nothing there right now. 

I really am trying my best to feel those things again, I promise. I don't want to be like this. Did that... does that answer what you were asking? I just want to make sure I'm giving you what you need.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
I... oh dear, I am so incredibly sorry. I feel terrible that you've had to ask me this again. I must be doing such a horrible job of explaining myself, and I really, really don't want to waste your time or be a difficult patient. I promise I'm trying to be helpful.

But... yes, yes, it is most of the day, almost every single day. I think... I think I just haven't been saying it clearly enough, but it's really the main reason I finally made the appointment. I just feel like... like the warm part of me completely shut off. 

Even when I'm just sitting on the couch with my husband, or trying to watch my favorite shows after a long shift at the store... I know I should be feeling some comfort, or at least a little bit of happiness, because I always used to. But right now... there's just nothing there. It's like I'm empty inside. 

I'm trying so hard to feel those things again, I really am. I don't want to be like this. Am I... am I making sense now? Please tell me if I'm not giving you the right answer, I just want to make sure I'm doing this correctly for you.
